[[status-register]]
STATUS register

* Base Offset: 0x10
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|======================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name

|======================================

[[empty-field]]
empty field
+++++++++++

0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read

[[full-field]]
full field
++++++++++

0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write

[[write_level_above_thresh-field]]
write_level_above_thresh field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

If set, write fifo level is higher than threshhold set in WIRQT

[[read_level_above_thresh-field]]
read_level_above_thresh field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

If set, read fifo level is higher than threshhold set in RIRQT

[[write_error-field]]

[[wirqt-register]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x20
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x28
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[irqs-register]]
IRQS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x30
* Base Offset: 0x30
* Size: 0x8

This register is used to read and clear interrupt requests, regardless
of IRQEN register and whether the interrupt is enabled or disabled.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |wtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|1 |rtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|2 |eirq |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wtirq-field]]
wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[rtirq-field]]
rtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in read
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[eirq-field]]
eirq field
++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Error on mailbox access On write:
link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No acknowledge
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt
request

[[empty-field-3]]
empty field
+++++++++++

0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read

[[full-field-3]]
full field
++++++++++

0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write

[[write_level_above_thresh-field-3]]
write_level_above_thresh field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

If set, write fifo level is higher than threshhold set in WIRQT

[[read_level_above_thresh-field-3]]
read_level_above_thresh field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

If set, read fifo level is higher than threshhold set in RIRQT

[[wirqt-register-3]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-3]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-3]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-3]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[irqs-register-3]]
IRQS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1830
* Base Offset: 0x30
* Size: 0x8

This register is used to read and clear interrupt requests, regardless
of IRQEN register and whether the interrupt is enabled or disabled.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |wtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|1 |rtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|2 |eirq |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wtirq-field-9]]
wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[rtirq-field-9]]
rtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in read
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[eirq-field-9]]
eirq field
++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Error on mailbox access On write:
link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No acknowledge
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt
request

[[wirqt-register-6]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x3020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-6]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-6]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x3028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-6]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No

[[wirqt-register-9]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x4820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-9]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-9]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x4828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-9]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-12]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-16]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x8020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-16]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-16]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x8028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-16]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-19]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x9820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-19]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-19]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x9828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-19]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[irqs-register-19]]
IRQS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Base Offset: 0x30
* Size: 0x8

of IRQEN register and whether the interrupt is enabled or disabled.

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |wtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|1 |rtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|2 |eirq |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wtirq-field-57]]
wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[rtirq-field-57]]
rtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in read
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[eirq-field-57]]
eirq field
++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Error on mailbox access On write:
link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No acknowledge
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt
request

[[wirqt-register-22]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xB020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-22]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-22]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xB028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-22]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wtirq-field-66]]
wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

rtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request

mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[eirq-field-66]]
eirq field
++++++++++

link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt
request

read_error field

[[write_error-field-25]]
write_error field
+++++++++++++++++

[[wirqt-register-25]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xC820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-25]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-25]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xC828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-25]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-field-28]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-32]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-32]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-32]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-32]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-35]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x11820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-35]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-35]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x11828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-35]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear

++++++++++

request

[[wirqt-register-38]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x13020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-38]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-38]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x13028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-38]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

Attempted read from an empty mailbox

[[write_error-field-41]]
write_error field
+++++++++++++++++

Attempted write to a full mailbox

[[wirqt-register-41]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x14820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-41]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-41]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x14828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-41]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-45]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x16820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-45]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-45]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-45]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-48]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x18020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-48]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-48]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x18028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-48]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

++++++++++

link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt
request

[[wirqt-register-51]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x19820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-51]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-51]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x19828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-51]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[wirqt-register-54]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1B020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-54]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-54]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1B028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-54]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request

mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No

interrupt request

* Absolute Address: 0x1C820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-57]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-57]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1C828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-57]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.

[[irqs-register-57]]

^^^^^^^^^^^^^

of IRQEN register and whether the interrupt is enabled or disabled.

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |wtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|1 |rtirq |rw |0x0 |—
|2 |eirq |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

wtirq field
+++++++++++

On read: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No interrupt request
link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Usage level threshold in write
mailbox exceeded On write: link:No%20error%20IRQ%20pending[0]: No
acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

rtirq field

acknowledge link:Error%20IRQ%20pending%3C/p%3E[1]: Acknowledge and clear
interrupt request

[[wirqt-register-61]]
WIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1E820
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |wirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[wirqt-field-61]]
wirqt field
+++++++++++

When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds
this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is
full.

[[rirqt-register-61]]
RIRQT register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1E828
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x8

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rirqt |rw |0x0 |—
|=====================================

[[rirqt-field-61]]
rirqt field
+++++++++++

When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds
this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding
STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the
MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to
MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is
full.
